Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in innate theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 734 and the invariant I(A,B) = 626 mod 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to innate theory, we decompose the problem into 734 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 626. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
